## AQL query templates

```aql
FOR d IN declarations
  FILTER d.socket_debt_tag == true
  LET owners = (
    FOR e IN edges
      FILTER e._to == d._id AND e.kind == "owns_socket"
      RETURN e
  )
  FILTER LENGTH(owners) == 0
  RETURN {
    name: d.name,
    module: d.module,
    rep_depth: d.rep_depth,
    socket_debt_class: d.socket_debt_class
  }
```

```aql
FOR d IN declarations
  FILTER d.owner_target_tag == true
  COLLECT h = d.type_hash_alpha INTO group
  FILTER LENGTH(group) > 1
  RETURN {
    type_hash_alpha: h,
    declarations: group[*].d.name,
    modules: group[*].d.module
  }
```

```aql
FOR d IN declarations
  FILTER d.template_role IN ["Gate", "Bridge", "Socket", "Witness"]
  FILTER d.socket_debt_tag != true AND d.owner_target_tag != true AND d.bridge_target_tag != true
  RETURN {
    name: d.name,
    module: d.module,
    template_role: d.template_role,
    status: "untagged_template_object"
  }
```
